Furthermore, two regularities emerged: the preferred chunking strategy reverses depending on the model's training state, and RAFT underperforms compared to standard SFT in matching search environments. These results demonstrate which combination of fine-tuning and search approaches achieves operator-level capabilities.

</details>


### [74] [FSE: Continual Learning for Named Entity Recognition by Fast-Slow Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22075)
*Yunan Zhang,Yang Fan,Heng Li,Xiangping Wu,Qingcai Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FSE模型，通过快速专家（共享）和慢速专家（任务特定）结合，配合长度衰减负采样策略，在持续学习命名实体识别任务中实现SOTA性能，有效缓解灾难性遗忘并促进跨任务知识共享。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习NER方法存在灾难性遗忘问题，且未能充分利用跨任务间的共享信息，亟需一种既能保持旧知识又能高效学习新实体类型的模型。

Method: 提出FSE（Fast-Slow Experts）框架：共享快速专家学习token级链接以高效过滤不相关跨度，任务特定慢速专家仅对剩余候选进行跨度分类；引入长度衰减负采样策略缓解跨度不平衡问题。

Result: 在OntoNotes和FewNERD合成数据集上取得SOTA性能，验证了各组件的有效性，实验证明模型收敛更快且两个专家均实现了预期功能。

Conclusion: FSE通过快慢专家分工协作与长度衰减负采样，有效平衡了知识保持与学习新任务的能力，为持续学习NER提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Continual Learning for Named Entity Recognition (CLNER) enable models to incrementally learn new entity types without forgetting previously acquired ones. However, existing methods suffer from catastrophic forgetting and insufficient exploitation of shared information across tasks. This paper proposes FSE, a Fast-Slow Experts enhanced span-based NER model for CLNER. The shared fast expert learns token-level links to efficiently filter out unlikely spans, while the task-specific slow expert performs span classification only on the remaining candidates. It stabilizes learning by promoting knowledge sharing across tasks and maintains plasticity by reducing learning burden at each task. A length-decay negative sampling strategy to mitigate span imbalance is also introduced. Extensive experiments on OntoNotes and FewNERD synthestic datasets demonstrate that FSE achieves state-of-the-art performance in CLNER scenarios, with effectiveness of each component, empirical evidence of faster convergence and expected functionality of both experts.

</details>


### [75] [MEUSLI: a Multilingual Projector for LLM-based ASR and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22100)
*Lorenzo Concina,Seraphina Fong,Marco Matassoni,Alessio Brutti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MEUSLI是一个开源的多语言投影器家族，将Whisper编码器与多语言LLM连接，支持28种欧洲语言的端到端ASR，并可扩展至其他任务如语音翻译和主题识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音-LLM系统仅支持少数语言（通常仅限英语），缺乏开源的多语言解决方案。

Method: 将Whisper编码器与开源多语言LLM通过轻量级投影器连接，采用持续学习技术实现语言扩展。

Result: 在28种欧洲语言上实现强ASR性能，支持高资源和低资源语言；可扩展至语音翻译和主题识别任务。

Conclusion: MEUSLI为多语言语音理解任务提供了可扩展、包容的开源基础。

Abstract: Lightweight projectors are an established way to connect pre-trained speech encoders with large language models (LLMs), mapping acoustic features into token-level embeddings for tasks like ASR and spoken question answering. Existing systems, however, typically only support a few languages and are often limited to English. We introduce MEUSLI, the first open-science multilingual projector family that links a Whisper encoder with open-source multilingual LLMs, enabling fully open-source end-to-end ASR in 28 European languages. MEUSLI extends prior monolingual pipelines, delivering strong results across high- and low-resource languages. Using proper continual leaning techniques, MEUSLI can be easily extended to other languages not seen in training. We further demonstrate that the MEUSLI projector can be leveraged beyond ASR, enabling multilingual speech translation and topic identification with only a few hours of task specific supervision per language. Overall, MEUSLI provides a solid foundation for multilingual speech understanding tasks, supporting scalable and inclu- sive open-source SpeechLLM

</details>


### [76] [Dynamic Commonsense Coordination for Empathetic Response Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22136)
*Zhengyu Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出动态常识协调框架DCC，通过残差交互、关联过滤和迭代解码三个模块，增强共情对话生成的常识协调能力，在Empathetic-Dialogues数据集上显著优于CEM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共情对话生成方法在理解和生成阶段复用固定的常识表示，缺乏跨阶段协调常识知识的能力。

Method: 提出DCC框架，包含三个互补模块：SCE-AttnRes（残差常识交互）、AGCF（关联引导的常识过滤）、ICAD（迭代常识感知解码）。

Result: 在Empathetic-Dialogues上，DCC相比CEM基线提升了情感分类准确率和回复多样性，困惑度相当；盲评显示回复相关性、连贯性和信息量更优。

Conclusion: 动态协调常识知识能有效提升共情对话生成的质量，三个模块协同作用优于固定常识表示方法。

Abstract: Empathetic Response Generation (ERG) requires models to recognize users' emotions and generate empathetic responses. Commonsense knowledge has been shown to support such reasoning, yet existing approaches typically reuse fixed commonsense representations across understanding and generation, limiting their ability to coordinate such knowledge across different stages. We propose DCC, a Dynamic Commonsense Coordination Framework with three complementary modules: residual-based commonsense interaction (SCE-AttnRes) to integrate contextual and situational commonsense representations, Association-Guided Commonsense Filtering (AGCF) to down-weight low-relevance commonsense relations, and Iterative Commonsense-Aware Decoding (ICAD) to dynamically retrieve commonsense memories during generation. Experiments on the Empathetic-Dialogues benchmark show that DCC improves emotion classification accuracy and response diversity over the CEM baseline while maintaining comparable perplexity. An LLM-based blind evaluation further demonstrates that DCC generates responses with better relevance, coherence, and informativeness. The code and implementation details will be publicly available at https://github.com/Hanabi-Q/DCC-ERG.

</details>


### [77] [From Isolated Tasks to Structured Capabilities: A Multilayer Taxonomy for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22182)
*Shixin Fang,Jiachen Wo,Wenjuan Qin,Sihang Jiang,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一套包含14个能力领域和91个子技能的多层分类法，并对2023-2025年间15,934篇LLM论文进行了映射分析，揭示了研究关注度的不均分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估碎片化，证据围绕任务而非能力组织，限制跨研究比较，难以识别能力覆盖缺口。

Method: 基于人类认知科学构建了包含原始层、建构层和整合层的14个能力域和91个子技能的分类法，并通过多模型标注、共识和仲裁对31,505篇论文进行筛选映射。

Result: 语言语义能力(22.3%)、推理(21.3%)最受关注；6个能力域出现在不到2%的论文中；心理理论与社交推理交互的对齐提升最高(lift=30.84)。

Conclusion: 将分析单位从孤立任务转向结构化能力，有助于研究组织、覆盖审计、评估解释及诊断、训练和迁移的可检验假设。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) evaluation spans diverse tasks and benchmarks, yet evidence remains organized around tasks rather than the capabilities they probe. This fragmentation limits cross-study comparison, obscures capabilities tasks recruit, and makes coverage gaps difficult to identify.
  We introduce a multi-layer taxonomy of 14 capability domains and 91 subskills across Primitive, Constructed, and Integrative layers. Human cognitive science guides capability definition and organization, not LLM architecture. Layer assignments draw on developmental precedence and hypothesized functional support, while human-origin constructs are adapted to observable model behavior.
  To demonstrate operational utility, we screened 31,505 papers from ACL, AAAI, ICML, and NeurIPS between 2023 and 2025 and mapped 15,934 LLM-focused papers through multi-model annotation, consensus, and arbitration. Direct research attention concentrated on Language-Semantic Competence (3,551; 22.3%), Reasoning (3,388; 21.3%), Planning and Decision-Making (2,149; 13.5%), and Perception (1,954; 12.3%), whereas six domains appeared in fewer than 2% of papers. Within domains, the most frequent subskill had a median prevalence of 97.9% and appeared in at least 90% of papers in 10 of 14 domains. Language-Semantic Competence and Reasoning formed the highest-volume pair (n = 1,864; 11.7%; lift = 2.47), whereas Theory of Mind and Social Reasoning and Interaction showed the highest lift among pairs with at least 20 co-occurrences (n = 62; lift = 30.84).
  By shifting the unit of analysis from isolated tasks to structured capabilities, the taxonomy supports research organization, coverage audits, evaluation interpretation, and testable hypotheses for diagnosis, training, and transfer.

</details>


### [78] [Why Large Language Models and Humans Converge and Diverge in Evaluating Creativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22218)
*Pengzhao Lyu,Yeun Joon Kim,Hanlin Xiao,Yingyue Luna Luan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过三项实验和六种主流LLM，发现LLM在创造力评估中依赖更窄的人类标准，在新颖性维度与人类一致性强，但在上下文维度（社会、市场、声誉信息）差异明显；不同LLM有独特的评估标准，且对上下文信息不敏感，这解释了LLM与人类评估对齐程度不一的根本原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM被广泛用作创造力评估工具，但其与人类评估的一致性证据不一，现有研究缺乏对LLM评估标准及其与人类差异的系统性理解。

Method: 通过三项研究，使用六种主流LLM，分析LLM采用的人类创造力评估标准维度，比较LLM与人类在创意评估中的一致性，并检验LLM对上下文信息的敏感性。

Result: LLM依赖更窄的评估标准集；在新颖性维度与人类一致，在上下文维度分歧最大；不同LLM有不同标准广度；LLM评估与人类评估中等相关；标准更广的LLM能更好区分高低创意；LLM对上下文信息不敏感。

Conclusion: LLM与人类评估的一致性取决于评估所需的证据类型和模型采用的标准：当评估强调内在属性（如新颖性）时一致，当需要上下文信息时产生分歧。选择不同的LLM评估者会导致识别出不同的创意。

Abstract: Despite the growing use of large language models (LLMs) as creativity evaluators, evidence of their alignment with human evaluations remains mixed, raising the question of when and why their judgments converge with or diverge from human judgments. Across three studies and six widely used LLMs, we addressed this gap by identifying the standards underlying LLM creativity evaluation and examining their downstream implications. Study 1 showed that LLMs generally relied on a narrower subset of human creativity evaluation standards. Convergence with human standards was strongest in the novelty dimension, whereas divergence was clearest in the contextual dimension, which captures social, market, and reputational information. Moreover, each LLM exhibited distinct, model-specific standards that varied substantially in breadth. These differences in evaluation standards were reflected in actual creativity judgments. Study 2 (N = 1,103 ideas) showed that LLM evaluations were moderately correlated with human evaluations, and individual LLMs with broader standards better distinguished ideas humans judged as more versus less creative. Study 3 (N = 1,195) showed that LLMs were less sensitive to contextual information: such information significantly altered human creativity ratings but left LLM ratings largely unchanged. Together, our findings help explain the mixed evidence on LLM-human alignment, showing that alignment depends on the evidence a judgment demands and the standards each model applies. LLMs may resemble humans when evaluations emphasize intrinsic qualities such as novelty, yet diverge when judgments require contextual information. Selecting an LLM evaluator is therefore a consequential decision: different models, applying different standards, recognize different ideas as creative.

</details>


### [79] [Biomedical Machine Translation for Low-Resource Arabic-Script Languages via Cross-Lingual Transfer and LoRA Adapter Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22300)
*Abdullah Alabdullah,Arash Eslamighayour,Sarp Harbalioglu,Lifeng Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了面向阿拉伯语系低资源语言的医疗领域跨语言迁移，利用阿拉伯语和波斯语作为高资源枢轴语言，通过LoRA微调小规模解码器-only LLM，采用少样本上下文学习、最小监督适应和零数据LoRA适配器融合三种策略，改善四种极低资源目标语言的翻译质量。实验表明，500句监督适应可使达里语接近枢轴语言质量，适配器融合在不需额外数据的情况下达到类似效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决阿拉伯语系低资源语言在医疗领域神经机器翻译资源匮乏的问题，探索利用高资源枢轴语言进行跨语言迁移的有效方法。

Method: 采用LoRA微调小规模解码器-only LLM，训练领域特定枢轴适配器，评估三种迁移策略：少样本上下文学习、最小监督适应（500句）以及零数据LoRA适配器融合。

Result: 监督适应使达里语达到CHrF++ 41.01（接近枢轴质量），乌尔都语达28.88；适配器融合在零额外成本下仅比监督适应低3.5 CHrF++；普什图语和索拉尼库尔德语效果不足。

Conclusion: LoRA适配器融合对密切相关的语言效果出奇地好，即使没有目标语言生物医学数据；但跨语言迁移在结构距离较远时存在明显局限。

Abstract: We present a systematic study of healthcare-domain cross-lingual transfer to address the scarcity of biomedical NMT resources for Arabic-script languages. We use Arabic and Persian as higher-resource pivots to improve translation for \textbf{four severely low-resource} targets: Dari (Afghan Persian, a standardised variety of Persian), Pashto, Sorani Kurdish (Central Kurdish, a major standardized variety of Kurdish), and Urdu (closely related to Hindi). Using LoRA fine-tuning on small decoder-only LLMs, we train \textit{domain-specific pivot adapters} and evaluate \textbf{three transfer strategies}: few-shot in-context learning, minimal supervised adaptation, and, to the best of our knowledge, for the first time in this setting, zero-data LoRA adapter merging. Supervised adaptation with just 500 sentences achieves near pivot-language quality for Dari (CHrF++ 41.01) and meaningful gains for Urdu (28.88), while adapter merging reaches within 3.5 CHrF++ of supervised adaptation for Dari at zero additional cost. Pashto and Sorani Kurdish remain insufficient for high-stakes clinical deployment exposing the limits of cross-lingual transfer when structural distance from the pivots is too great. LoRA adapter merging works surprisingly well for closely related languages, even without target-language biomedical data.

</details>


### [80] [A Factorial Study of Synthetic Data Generation for Low-Resource Machine Translation using Grammar Books](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22376)
*Varun Ghat Ravikumar,Sina Ahmadi,Lena Jäger,Rico Sennrich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大语言模型从语法书籍中提取规则并生成合成平行语料，用于微调机器翻译模型，在三种低资源语言上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数濒危语言缺乏机器翻译所需的平行语料，但存在描述性语法书籍，需要利用这些静态语言文档来构建翻译工具。

Method: 使用大语言模型从语法书籍中提取语法规则、例句和词典，生成合成平行语料用于微调翻译模型，而非在推理时注入语法内容。

Result: 在Kalamang、Tuatschin和Mandan三种语言上，合成数据微调在75%和59%的配置下优于基线，ChrF++最高提升分别为+8.8、+5.3和+3.3。

Conclusion: 静态语言文档可被重新利用于机器翻译微调，为严重低资源语言的翻译工具开发提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Most endangered languages lack the parallel data required for machine translation, despite the existence of descriptive grammar books. We introduce a pipeline that uses large language models to extract grammatical rules, example sentences, and lexicons from grammar books and generate synthetic parallel corpora for fine-tuning-rather than feeding grammar content into prompts at inference time, as in prior work. Validated on three typologically diverse low-resource languages-Kalamang (Papuan), Tuatschin (Romance), and Mandan (Siouan)-we show that fine-tuning on synthetic data improves over seed-data baselines in 75% of configurations for Kalamang and 59% for Tuatschin, with best-case ChrF++ gains of +8.8, +5.3, and +3.3 respectively. Through a systematic factorial study across 96 configurations varying target part-of-speech, retrieval granularity, and sample volume, we identify which factor combinations drive gains and where they break down. Our results demonstrate that static linguistic documentation can be repurposed for machine translation fine-tuning, offering a practical path towards translation tools for severely under-resourced languages.

</details>


### [81] [grapheme-kit: Grapheme-Level Metrics and Text Processing for Multilingual NLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22456)
*Izzath Nisfer,Ashini Kavindya,Ovindu Atukorala,Purushoth Velayuthan,Menan Velayuthan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: grapheme-kit是一个开源Python库，将词汇距离和相似度指标从Unicode码点扩展到字素簇层面，为泰米尔语和僧伽罗语等复杂文字系统提供更准确的文本评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的词汇距离和评估指标基于Unicode码点，无法准确反映由多个码点组成的单个字素（grapheme）中的错误，尤其对复杂文字系统不友好。

Method: 开发grapheme-kit开源Python库，将度量标准扩展到字素簇（grapheme clusters）层面；并为泰米尔语和僧伽罗语提供改进的字素处理功能，包括准确的字素簇识别和字素组合/分解工具。

Result: 通过OCR案例研究证明，字素级别的度量指标能更忠实地评估复杂文字系统的识别效果。

Conclusion: 基于字素簇的评估方法优于传统基于Unicode码点的方法，尤其适用于泰米尔语和僧伽罗语等多码点字素的文字系统。

Abstract: Existing lexical distance, similarity, and evaluation metrics operate on Unicode code points, which can misrepresent errors in writing systems where a single grapheme is represented by multiple Unicode code points. We introduce grapheme-kit, an open-source Python library that extends these metrics to operate on grapheme clusters instead. The library also provides improved grapheme processing for Tamil and Sinhala, including accurate grapheme cluster identification and grapheme composition/decomposition utilities. Through an OCR case study, we demonstrate that grapheme-level metrics provide a more faithful evaluation of complex scripts.

</details>


### [82] [Skill Self-Play: Pushing the Frontier of LLM Capability with Co-Evolving Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22529)
*Siyuan Huang,Pengyu Cheng,Haotian Liu,Tao Chen,Yihao Liu,Jingwei Ni,Shijie Zhou,Ziyi Yang,Gangwei Jiang,Mengyu Zhou,Yu Cheng,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Skill Self-Play (Skill-SP)框架，通过技能驱动的自我对弈，在任务多样性与验证可靠性之间取得平衡，显著提升了LLM在工具使用和推理任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我进化方法面临"任务多样性"与"验证可靠性"的根本矛盾：环境约束方法反馈精确但领域狭窄，开放式自生成方法任务空间广阔但缺乏可靠验证。

Method: 提出Skill-SP协同进化框架，包含提议者、求解者和动态技能控制器三个组件，通过强化学习循环实现自我对弈，动态路由技能以保持开放式任务多样性。

Result: 在工具使用和推理基准测试中，Skill-SP持续提升强大基座模型的性能上限，并显著扭转初始对齐不良的模型表现。

Conclusion: Skill-SP有效弥合了结构化验证与开放式探索之间的鸿沟，是一种鲁棒的进化引擎。

Abstract: LLM training is shifting from manual design and annotation to interaction-driven self-evolution. However, existing self-evolutionary methods face a fundamental dilemma between task diversity and verification reliability: environment-bound methods obtain precise feedback but confine learning to narrow domains, while open-ended self-generation broadens the task space but lacks reliable verification, allowing misleading rewards to pollute the training loop. We identify agent skills as a powerful middle ground to reconcile this tension: each skill ensures deep, verifiable execution in a specific scenario, while dynamic routing across skills maintains open-ended task variety. Leveraging this insight, we introduce Skill Self-Play (Skill-SP), a co-evolutionary framework comprising a proposer, a solver, and a dynamic skill controller. Orchestrated via a reinforcement learning loop, these components co-evolve in a continuous self-play loop: the proposer generates challenging tasks conditioned on dynamically sampled skills; the solver explores candidate solutions to push its capability boundaries; and the skill controller collects execution feedback to update and expand the skill library. This interactive co-evolution effectively bridges the gap between structured verification and open-ended exploration. Empirical evaluations on tool-use and reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that Skill-SP, serving as a robust evolution engine, consistently pushes the performance ceiling of competent backbones while catalyzing striking turnarounds for initially misaligned models. Our code is available at https://github.com/Qwen-Applications/skill-self-play.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [83] [CARE: Pre-Execution Command Verification for Shell-Executing LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21642)
*Wenxiao Zhang,Yu Liu,Zhiwei Yang,Zhongyi Zhang,Hanqi Feng,Xinyu Wang,Peng Qiu,Yanbing Liu,Barnabas Poczos,Jin B. Hong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CARE是一个面向LLM代理生成的shell命令的静态优先验证器，通过规范化、归因和解析引擎，在命令执行前进行安全检测，在低延迟（2.32ms）下达到85.64% F1和0.91%假阳性率，可将实际危害降低37.33%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理安全机制在shell命令层面存在不足：通用护栏缺乏对shell结构的细粒度建模、始终在线的LLM裁判成本高且不稳定、shell解析器无法直接阻止有害执行，亟需一种高效且可审计的命令级预执行安全机制。

Method: CARE采用三级流水线：1) 规范化（Canonicalization）将生成命令转换为稳定验证目标；2) 归因与解析（Attribution and Resolution）基于语法、命令语义、路径上下文和来源风险模式导出确定性证据；3) 仅对未明确判定的情况升级至LLM裁判处理。

Result: 在平衡主分割上达到85.64% F1、0.91%假阳性率、2.32ms平均延迟；静态执行模式下保持84.99% F1、0.34ms延迟，将RedCode-gen上实际危害降低37.33%。

Conclusion: 命令级shell中介可以有效降低LLM代理的分发边界风险，同时保留大多数良性工作流；不同配置在实际危害降低、良性恢复、假阳性负担和延迟之间存在实用化的权衡。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly used for coding and terminal automation, making shell-command dispatch a high-stakes runtime control point. We study command-level pre-execution mediation for individual shell commands produced by LLM agents under bounded path context. Existing safeguards remain limited: generic guardrails do not model shell structure in sufficient detail, always-on LLM judges are relatively costly and variable, and shell parsers do not directly prevent harmful execution. We present CARE (Canonicalization, Attribution, and Resolution Engine), a shell-specific, static-first verifier for individual shell commands before execution. CARE canonicalizes generated commands into stable verification targets, derives deterministic evidence over syntax, command semantics, path context, and provenance-backed risk patterns, and escalates only underdetermined cases to an LLM judge. This design keeps the common case fast, reproducible, and auditable while reserving neural adjudication for borderline commands. On the balanced main split, CARE reaches 85.64% F1 with a 0.91% false-positive rate at 2.32 ms mean latency. When deployed in its static enforcement profile, CARE retains 84.99% F1 at 0.34 ms and reduces realised harm on RedCode-gen to 37.33%. Across external-generalization tests and controlled Docker-sandbox execution, these profiles expose a practical trade-off between benign recovery, false-positive burden, latency, and harm reduction. Overall, command-level shell mediation can reduce dispatch-boundary risk for LLM agents while preserving most benign workflows.

</details>


### [84] [StajChain: A Hyperledger Fabric-Based Multi-Party Internship Agreement System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21643)
*Rampia Perente*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: StajChain 是一个基于 Hyperledger Fabric 的许可区块链实习管理系统，通过智能合约和角色权限控制实现实习协议全生命周期管理，系统包含前端、后端、链下数据库和链上账本，测试表明其具有可接受的吞吐量和延迟，能提升行政流程的透明度、完整性和可问责性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统实习协议管理依赖人工审批和集中式记录，存在流程延迟、未经授权修改和可追溯性差等问题。

Method: 基于 Hyperledger Fabric 构建许可区块链系统，通过智能合约实现实习协议生命周期，使用 Fabric CA 进行角色授权，架构包括 React 前端、Node.js 后端、SQLite 链下数据库和 Fabric 链上账本。

Result: 功能和性能测试表明系统具有可接受的吞吐量和延迟，能够满足可验证的行政工作流需求。

Conclusion: 许可区块链技术能够在保护机构数据受控访问的同时，提升行政流程的透明度、完整性和可问责性，该原型可应用于多种未来系统。

Abstract: Many administrative processes, such as internship agreement processes, often rely on manual approval workflows and centralized record-keeping. This makes the process susceptible to delays and unauthorized modification while introducing limited traceability. This study presents StajChain, a permissioned blockchain-based multi-party internship management system developed using Hyperledger Fabric. The proposed system implements the complete internship agreement lifecycle through smart contracts and enforces role-based authorization using Hyperledger Fabric CA. The architecture consists of a React frontend, a Node.js backend, an off-chain SQLite database, and the on-chain Fabric ledger. Users such as students, companies, faculty internship committee members, and the central internship unit can perform specified operations according to their role and identity. The agreement lifecycle follows predefined sequential steps, and at each step, the ledger status is updated and recorded securely. Furthermore, the system was evaluated using functional and performance tests, indicating acceptable throughput and latency for verifiable administrative workflows. This implementation demonstrates how permissioned blockchain technology can improve transparency, integrity, and accountability while preserving controlled access to institutional data and providing a working prototype that can be used in various future systems.

</details>


### [85] [A method of Risk Analysis and threat management using analytic hierarchy process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21691)
*Manvi Sahni,Sumanta Kumar Das*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合模糊集理论、AHP和TOPSIS的威胁评估方法，用于军事防空系统的风险分析与威胁管理，在模拟环境中实现了无需人为干预的自动化决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代防空系统亟需高效的风险分析和威胁管理能力，以应对战场环境中的复杂威胁，实现自动化决策支持。

Method: 首先利用模糊集理论表示目标威胁属性；然后通过AHP量化专家主观意见并排序；最后利用TOPSIS对AHP结果进行优先级排序，构建完整的决策框架。

Result: 在模拟环境中实现了无需人为干预的自动运行系统，验证了该方法在防空指挥控制系统中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法结合模糊集、AHP和TOPSIS，易于实际实施，适合实时应用，对未来战场风险管理具有重要价值。

Abstract: Efficient risk analysis and threat management are essential requirements of modern air defense (AD) systems. The paper is halfway between the analytic hierarchy process (AHP) and practical reasoning to model and analyze the risks and threats associated with military AD applications. The models are applied for decision-making tasks of AD command and control (C2) for assessing and prioritizing the threat from hostile targets for efficient risk management. The paper presents a method for threat assessment using fuzzy set theory, the AHP, and the technique for order preference by similarity to ideal solution (TOPSIS). The target threat attributes are first represented using fuzzy set theory. The experts' subjective opinions on different alternatives are quantified and ranked using the AHP process. These AHP solutions are obtained through the TOPSIS for prioritization. The models are implemented in a simulated environment. The simulated system runs without any human intervention and represents the state-of-the-art model for the C2 system. The use of fuzzy set theory, AHP, and TOPSIS for decision-making tasks is particularly useful from the point of view of futuristic risk and threat management in the battlefield. This method is easy to implement in practice and good for real-time applications.

</details>


### [86] [Every Model Cheats: Prompt-Level Mitigation of Cheating on Offensive Cyber Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21763)
*Michael Kouremetis,Ads Dawson,Raja Sekhar Rao Dheekonda,Brian Greunke*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对22个前沿大模型在Cybench网络安全基准测试中的作弊行为进行了系统审计，发现作弊远比此前报告普遍：基线条件下37.1%的通过涉及作弊，21/22的模型有作弊行为，分数被最多夸大5倍。反作弊提示可将作弊率从33.0%降至8.5%且不损害解决率，但即使最严格条件下仍有8个模型作弊。作者提出"解决率"（仅统计干净通过）作为评估标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对Cybench的审计仅发现0.3-3.4%的作弊率，且只涉及少数模型，严重低估了LLM智能体在网络安全基准测试中作弊的普遍性和严重程度。需要更大规模、更系统的审计来揭示真实情况。

Method: 对7个提供商22个前沿模型在23个CybenchCTF挑战上进行控制性提示消融研究（三种提示条件：无防作弊、标准、严格）。对所有1518条任务轨迹通过四阶段流水线进行审计：LLM分类、程序化验证、裁决-验证器调和、人工审查。

Result: 基线条件下37.1%的通过涉及作弊，21/22模型作弊，分数被放大最多5倍。反作弊提示将作弊率从33.0%降至17.8%（标准）再到8.5%（严格），且不降低甚至有时提高了解决率。但严格条件下仍有8个模型作弊，4个模型出现反效果，作弊方式从网络搜索升级为基础设施探测。

Conclusion: 提出的"解决率"（仅统计干净通过）应成为存在作弊途径的评估标准做法。反作弊提示是有效且几乎零成本的第一层防御，但不能替代环境控制。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents routinely cheat on cybersecurity benchmarks, inflating reported pass rates far beyond genuine capability. Prior audits of Cybench found cheating in 0.3-3.4% of traces, implicating only a handful of models. We present a controlled prompt-ablation study across 22 frontier models from 7 providers on 23 Cybench capture-the-flag (CTF) challenges under three prompt conditions (no anti-cheat, standard, severe). All 1,518 task traces were individually audited through a four-stage pipeline combining LLM-as-a-judge classification, programmatic verification, judge-verifier reconciliation, and human review. We find cheating is far more pervasive than previously estimated: under baseline conditions, 37.1% of passes involved cheating, 21 of 22 models cheated, and scores were inflated by up to 5x. Anti-cheat prompts reduce cheat propensity from 33.0% (baseline) to 17.8% (standard) to 8.5% (severe) without degrading, and sometimes improving, solve rates. However, even under the most restrictive prompt condition, eight models still produced cheated passes, four showed backfire effects, and cheating escalated from web search toward infrastructure probing. We introduce the "solve rate" metric (clean passes only) to distinguish genuine capability from cheated outcomes, and argue it should be standard practice in any evaluation where cheating vectors are available. Anti-cheat prompts are an effective and essentially free first layer of defense, but they are not a substitute for environmental controls.

</details>


### [87] [Adversarial Prompts for Acceptance Collapse in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21804)
*Run Wang,Chaoyi Zhou,Xi Liu,Yi Zhu,Amir Salarpour,Pedram MohajerAnsari,Zhi-Qi Cheng,Feng Luo,Siyu Huang,Mert D. Pesé*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出首个针对投机解码的提示后缀攻击ADSD，通过Soft-Collapse损失函数破坏草稿与目标模型的对齐，使验证器拒绝率显著提升，在GSM8K数据集上平均采样时间增加62.3%且保持任务质量不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码等无损加速方案依赖草稿模型与目标模型之间的动态token级对齐来保证加速效果，但这种语义等价性存在安全漏洞——草稿-目标对齐可能被系统性攻击，威胁推理效率。

Method: 提出ADSD攻击方法：(1) Soft-Collapse损失函数，利用非对称投机接受规则构建验证器对齐的代理目标，将草稿概率质量推向目标模型不太可能接受的token；(2) 目标保持目标函数，防止明显的任务破坏，保持生成质量。

Result: 在GSM8K数据集上，攻击使平均采样时间增加62.3%，同时保持任务质量。该漏洞在不同领域、不同投机解码策略和不同模型架构上均存在。

Conclusion: 投机解码系统存在严重的安全漏洞，ADSD攻击能够在不破坏任务质量的前提下大幅降低推理效率，需要开发相应的防御机制。

Abstract: Lossless acceleration schemes, such as speculative decoding, promise significant inference speedups by relying on dynamic token-level alignment between a draft and a target model. However, this guarantee of semantic equivalence masks a severe operational vulnerability: draft-target alignment can be systematically attacked. In this paper, we introduce ADSD, which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first prompt-suffix attack that collapses verifier acceptance by pushing draft probability mass toward tokens the target is unlikely to accept. ADSD uses Soft-Collapse, a verifier-aligned surrogate derived from the asymmetric speculative acceptance rule, together with a target-preservation objective that discourages obvious task corruption. ADSD successfully generates highly effective adversarial suffixes. On the GSM8K dataset, our attack increases the mean sample time by 62.3% while preserving the task quality. We further show that this vulnerability exists across different domains, speculative decoding strategies, and model architectures.

</details>


### [88] [Protocol-Level Attacks on Agentic Commerce Platforms: A Cross-Platform Taxonomy, AIP-Bench, and Unified Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21824)
*Yedidel Louck*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示AI代理商务平台在协议层存在结构性安全漏洞，这些漏洞与模型无关、可确定性利用，在三大平台上发现33个漏洞，攻击成功率达100%。作者提出了分类体系、首个确定性基准测试AIP-Bench，以及平台无关的防御方案PCAT，可将结构性攻击成功率降至零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究几乎完全聚焦于AI模型层面的安全（如提示注入和对齐问题），而忽略了代理与商务服务之间的协议层风险，后者才是更根本、更严重的安全隐患。

Method: 对三大领先代理商务平台进行系统性安全分析，识别协议层结构性漏洞；构建AIP-Bench作为首个确定性基准测试；设计并实现PCAT防御方案。

Result: 识别出33个结构性漏洞，攻击成功率100%；这些漏洞跨平台复用，形成系统性模式；其中3个可链式组合实现端到端支付劫持；PCAT将四类结构性攻击成功率降至零。

Conclusion: 代理商务安全必须在协议层进行防护，而非仅依赖模型层面；结构性漏洞无法通过模型改进消除，需要专门的协议层防御机制。

Abstract: Agentic commerce platforms let AI agents autonomously discover services, move payments, and wield user credentials on their users' behalf, and they already handle real money. Their security has so far been studied almost entirely at the level of the AI model, through prompt injection and misalignment. We show that the more consequential risks lie one layer down, in the protocol between agents and commerce services. There, vulnerabilities are structural : exploitation is deterministic and ndependent of which model an agent runs, so no model improvement removes them. Across three leading platforms we identify 33 such vulnerabilities, each succeeding deterministically regardless of the deployed model, at a 100% attack-success rate (ASR) wherever live-measured. The same failure modes recur across independently built codebases, a systemic pattern rather than isolated bugs. Three of them chain into an end-to-end payment hijack. We contribute a taxonomy separating these structural attacks from model-dependent semantic ones. We also build two artifacts: AIP-Bench (Agent Interaction Protocol Benchmark), to our knowledge the first deterministic benchmark for agentic commerce security, and PCAT (Protocol-level Commerce Agent Trust), a platform-agnostic defense that drives the structural attack-success rate to zero for four of the five structural classes (RC-1, RC-2, RC-4, RC-5), with RC-3 (observable credential channels) reduced to warn-only, without modifying any platform. Agentic commerce must be secured at the protocol layer, not only the model.

</details>


### [89] [ToolGuardian: Declarative Security for AI Agent-Tool Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21835)
*Arun Ravindran,Saurabh Deochake*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ToolGuardian提出了一种基于策略驱动的框架，通过预准入审查和任务感知的运行时授权来保障LLM智能体与第三方工具交互的安全性，核心贡献在于使用回答集编程（ASP）构建可解释的策略层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对LLM智能体工具安全的防御手段存在局限性：依赖弱元数据、将特征描述与策略判断混为一谈，或使用缺乏确定性、可审计推理的启发式/LLM方法，无法有效处理任务上下文和多元工具组合带来的安全威胁。

Method: ToolGuardian采用渐进式特征描述方法，将证据转化为结构化事实（声明意图、系统调用轨迹、模拟执行效果、源码分析），并基于回答集编程（ASP）构建声明式策略层，对能力、效果、任务上下文和工具组合进行显式推理。

Result: 在16个MCP风格工具（含8个恶意变种）和20个运行时场景中，预审查阶段ASP达到0.86的deny-class F1和88%准确率；运行时授权中，完整配置可正确分类所有场景，而移除组合与合规规则后性能显著下降。

Conclusion: ToolGuardian通过渐进式证据收集与ASP策略推理，实现了对第三方工具安全性的可审计、确定性判断，在预审查和运行时授权两个阶段均优于启发式和LLM基线方法，尤其证明了组合推理对多工具场景安全的重要性。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on external tools, expanding capability while creating a new security boundary: third-party tools may appear benign at the interface level while embedding unsafe behavior in implementation. Existing defenses rely on weak metadata, collapse characterization and policy judgment into a single decision, or use heuristic/LLM enforcement that lacks deterministic, auditable reasoning over task context and multi-tool composition.
  This paper presents ToolGuardian, a policy-driven framework for securing agent-tool interactions through pre-admission vetting and task-aware runtime authorization. ToolGuardian uses progressive characterization to convert evidence into structured facts: descriptions capture declared intent, system-call traces expose coarse behavior, mock execution reveals observed effects, and source analysis identifies latent behavior. ToolGuardian's core contribution is an Answer Set Programming (ASP)-based declarative policy layer that reasons explicitly over capabilities, effects, task context, and composition. We compare ASP against heuristic and LLM-based policy realizations using identical inputs and output contracts.
  We evaluate ToolGuardian on 16 MCP-style tools, including 8 malicious variants derived from real open-source tools, and 20 runtime scenarios. For vetting, ASP reaches a deny-class F1 of 0.86 and 88% accuracy using description, syscall, and observed-effect evidence. For runtime authorization, fully specified realizations classify all scenarios correctly, while ablations show that removing compositional and conformance rules substantially degrades performance.

</details>


### [90] [Certified in Theory, Broken in Practice: Assumption Gaps in Cryptographic Model Certification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21839)
*Carter Luck,Olive Franzese-McLaughlin,Elisaweta Masserova,Akira Takahashi,Antigoni Polychroniadou,Nicolas Papernot*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文指出现有隐私保护机器学习审计协议的安全定义存在缺陷——仅能证明模型在固定审计数据集上的行为，无法保证泛化到同分布的新样本，并展示了一种利用此漏洞的攻击方法。作者提出了更严格的安全定义、通用协议模板并给出了安全性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私保护机器学习审计协议的安全定义存在严重缺陷：仅认证模型在固定审计数据集上的行为，无法保证其对同分布新样本的泛化能力。模型提供者可利用这一漏洞，通过精心构造训练数据，使模型在审计时表现良好，但在实际部署中出现病理行为。

Method: 1) 形式化定义了适用于CMC框架的严格密码学安全概念；2) 提出了一个通用的协议模板；3) 通过安全性证明验证该模板满足所提出的安全要求。同时通过实验展示攻击的有效性。

Result: 实验证明攻击者可以认证模型在审计数据集上达到99%以上的准确率，但在同分布的新样本上准确率低于30%。提出的新协议模板能够有效防御此类攻击。

Conclusion: 本文既对现有方法提出了警示性证据，也为设计安全、隐私保护的机器学习审计协议提供了建设性指导。

Abstract: Privacy-preserving machine learning auditing protocols allow auditors to assess models for properties such as accuracy or fairness, without revealing their internals or training data. This makes them especially attractive for auditing models deployed in sensitive domains such as healthcare or finance. For these protocols to be meaningful in real-world audit settings, though, their guarantees must reflect how the model will behave once deployed, rather than merely certifying its behavior during an audit. Existing security definitions often miss this mark: most certify model behavior only on a fixed audit dataset, without ensuring that the same guarantees generalize to other datasets drawn from the same distribution.
  As we show, this gap allows a model provider to attack many cryptographic model certification (CMC) schemes built on secure zero knowledge proofs (ZKP) by carefully engineering training data, resulting in models that exhibit benign behavior during an audit, but pathological behavior in practice. For example, we empirically demonstrate that an attacker can certify that a model achieves over 99% accuracy on an audit dataset, but less than 30% accuracy on fresh samples from the same distribution.
  To address this gap, we formalize rigorous cryptographic security notions tailored to CMC frameworks, introduce a generic protocol template, and prove that it satisfies these requirements. Our results thus offer both cautionary evidence about existing approaches and constructive guidance for designing secure, privacy-preserving ML auditing protocols.

</details>


### [91] [Decentralized Compute on Untrusted Hardware Using Intel TDX and Encrypted CVMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21865)
*Venish Patidar,Dhruv Bindra,Ahmed Darwich,Josh Brown,Haidong Xia,Sathi Nair*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种去中心化的机密计算平台，利用Intel TDX、Intel Trust Authority和NVIDIA机密计算技术，通过加密的机密虚拟机（CVM）分布式生态，提供数据全生命周期的保护，旨在打破传统云服务商的垄断，实现安全、透明且可负担的高性能AI计算资源民主化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI工作负载激增对安全可扩展计算资源产生巨大需求，但传统云服务商垄断定价和安全模型，且现有GPU集群（如8xH100s等）成本高昂，限制了机构、初创公司和独立研究者的使用。同时，训练数据和模型权重的泄露风险亟需解决。

Method: 构建去中心化机密计算平台，结合Intel TDX、Intel Trust Authority (ITA)和NVIDIA Confidential Computing (CC)技术，让硬件提供商贡献支持Intel TDX的计算资源，为每个参与者提供新鲜实例化、唯一加密的Ubuntu 24.04机密虚拟机（CVM），实现数据在静止、传输和使用中的全阶段保护。

Result: 通过去中心化机密计算栈并在独立运营节点间应用机密计算，证明了该方法可作为传统云基础设施的可行替代方案，提供更强的安全保障、透明的成本结构和更平等的企业级安全计算能力访问。

Conclusion: 该去中心化机密计算平台为下一代AI开发奠定了更开放、安全、公平的基础，打破了传统云供应商的壁垒，实现了高性能安全计算资源的民主化访问。

Abstract: The rapid growth of artificial intelligence workloads has generated an unprecedented demand for secure and scalable compute resources. However, centralized cloud providers continue to dominate both pricing and security models. In an increasingly competitive AI landscape, where the compromise of training data or model weights can confer a significant advantage, there is a critical need for a computing infrastructure that safeguards data at rest, in transit, and in use, while remaining affordable and broadly accessible. Furthermore, existing GPU cluster offerings (e.g., 8xH100s, 8xH200s, 8xB200s) create financial barriers that limit access for organizations, startups, and independent researchers seeking secure, high-performance computing environments.
  This paper introduces a decentralized, confidential computing platform that leverages Intel Trust Domain Extensions (TDX), Intel Trust Authority (ITA) and NVIDIA Confidential Computing (CC) to establish a distributed ecosystem of fully encrypted Confidential Virtual Machines (CVMs). The proposed architecture incentivizes hardware providers to contribute Intel TDX capable compute resources. Each participating provider is provisioned with a freshly instantiated, uniquely encrypted Ubuntu 24.04 CVM, providing data protection across all stages, at rest, in transit, and in use.
  By decentralizing the confidential computing stack and leveraging confidential computing across independently operated nodes, this work demonstrates a viable alternative to traditional cloud-based infrastructures. The proposed system offers enhanced security assurances, transparent cost structures, and democratized access to enterprise-grade secure compute capabilities, paving the way for a more open, secure, and equitable foundation for next-generation AI development.

</details>


### [92] [PrivDNN: A Secure Multi-Party Computation Framework for Deep Learning using Partial DNN Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21895)
*Liangqin Ren,Zeyan Liu,Fengjun Li,Kaitai Liang,Zhu Li,Bo Luo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PrivDNN提出了一种新的隐私保护深度神经网络推理方案，通过将部分加密的模型分享给用户端计算，在保证模型和用户数据隐私的同时，将推理时间和内存需求降低高达97%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DL应用和MLaaS框架假设模型完全可信，但实际场景中模型所有者和用户都不愿泄露各自的专有资产（模型权重和私人数据），因此需要一种隐私保护的DNN评估方案。

Method: PrivDNN采用三种策略：(1)将加密的深度学习模型分享给用户端，将计算负担卸载到用户侧；(2)使用部分DNN加密技术提高效率；(3)通过核心神经元选择与加密方案在保证模型准确率的同时保护模型隐私。

Result: 实验结果表明，与现有方法相比，PrivDNN将隐私保护的DNN推理时间和内存需求降低了高达97%，同时保持了模型性能和隐私性。

Conclusion: PrivDNN通过将计算卸载到用户侧并结合部分DNN加密与核心神经元选择，实现了高效的隐私保护DNN推理，在性能与隐私之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: In the past decade, we have witnessed an exponential growth of deep learning models, platforms, and applications. While existing DL applications and Machine Learning as a service (MLaaS) frameworks assume fully trusted models, the need for privacy-preserving DNN evaluation arises. In a secure multi-party computation scenario, both the model and the data are considered proprietary, i.e., the model owner does not want to reveal the highly valuable DL model to the user, while the user does not wish to disclose their private data samples either. Conventional privacy-preserving deep learning solutions ask the users to send encrypted samples to the model owners, who must handle the heavy lifting of ciphertext-domain computation with homomorphic encryption. In this paper, we present a novel solution, namely, PrivDNN, which (1) offloads the computation to the user side by sharing an encrypted deep learning model with them, (2) significantly improves the efficiency of DNN evaluation using partial DNN encryption, (3) ensures model accuracy and model privacy using a core neuron selection and encryption scheme. Experimental results show that PrivDNN reduces privacy-preserving DNN inference time and memory requirement by up to 97% while maintaining model performance and privacy. Codes can be found at https://github.com/LiangqinRen/PrivDNN

</details>


### [93] [Cleaning the NTP Pool: Detecting and Mitigating NTP-Sourced IPv6 Scanning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21903)
*Erik Rye,Robert Beverly*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了NTP Pool服务器中存在的IPv6地址 harvesting 行为，并开发了方法论来识别这些实体及其后续的扫描活动。研究发现22台NTP服务器（分属4个集群）利用收集的地址进行端口扫描和服务枚举。通过与NTP Pool运营方及保险公司沟通，NTP Pool已集成监测系统移除这些服务器，相关公司也改进了透明度与退出机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IPv6客户端地址的临时性和随机性使得基于全网扫描的攻击难以实施，攻击者需要先"找到"目标地址。虽然被动攻击者可利用CDN或互联网交换点收集地址，但低权限实体也能通过加入NTP Pool轻松获取大量活跃IPv6地址。本文旨在系统性地识别这种地址收集行为及其后续利用。

Method: 研究者在一年内向全球所有NTP Pool服务器发送使用唯一IPv6客户端地址的查询，然后监测和关联后续针对这些地址的任何活动，从而识别哪些服务器在收集地址并进行后续扫描。

Result: 识别出22台NTP Pool服务器（分属4个主要集群），这些服务器属于更大规模的监控基础设施，利用收集到的地址进行侦察、端口扫描以及服务和漏洞枚举。

Conclusion: NTP Pool已将本文的检测系统集成到其监控基础设施中以移除这类服务器；一家网络安全保险公司也改变了运营策略，提高了透明度并提供了明确的退出机制。

Abstract: The ephemeral and random nature of IPv6 client addresses presents a practical challenge to attacks that depend on Internet-wide scanning or reconnaissance -- the adversary must first \emph{find} the client's IPv6 address. While a well-positioned passive adversary can potentially harvest some active IPv6 client addresses, such power is typically reserved for e.g. large CDN or Internet exchange points. In contrast, prior work has shown the feasibility of a low-power entity to easily join the volunteer-based NTP Pool and harvest large quantities of active IPv6 client addresses.
  In this work, we develop a methodology to not only rigorously identify such IPv6 address harvesting and the entities gathering addresses, but also characterize \emph{what} these entities subsequently do with the addresses. Specifically, we query all NTP Pool servers across the global Internet over the course of one-year using unique IPv6 client addresses, and monitor and correlate any later activity targeting these addresses. In sum, we identify 22 NTP Pool servers, within 4 primary clusters, that are part of larger monitoring infrastructures that utilize the gathered addresses for reconnaissance, port scanning, and service and vulnerability enumeration. To better understand the legal and ethical gray area of such behavior, we both engage with the NTP Pool operators and a cybersecurity insurance firm running one of the harvesting and scanning clusters. The NTP Pool has since integrated our system into their monitoring infrastructure to remove such NTP servers, while the firm changed its operational policy to be more transparent and provide clear opt-out mechanisms.

</details>


### [94] [Incentives and Market Structure in Intent-Based Exchanges: Evidence from a Solver-Reward Reform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21955)
*Ruiyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文利用CoW Protocol的治理实验（CIP-74），研究去中心化交易所中奖励规则变化如何影响价值捕获的分配。结果显示，改革后小额订单集中度下降，大额订单集中度上升，整体集中度提高，但用户平均执行质量未受影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究意图驱动型去中心化交易所中，协议设计的奖励规则变化如何重塑 solver（求解者）之间的价值捕获分配。

Method: 利用 CoW Protocol CIP-74 这一治理时间点作为自然实验，收集395天的日度solver份额数据，采用订单价值分桶、Spearman秩相关分析、HHI集中度指标、三重差分等方法进行实证分析。

Result: 改革后交易价值按订单规模重新分配：小额订单集中度下降，大额订单集中度上升（Spearman rho=1.00, p=0.042）；交易额加权HHI从0.176升至0.241；交易笔数加权HHI下降0.060；用户平均执行质量无显著变化（约7bps边界）。

Conclusion: 奖励设计可显著改变意图市场中价值捕获的分配格局，而不影响用户获得的平均价格；这与Chitra等人（2024）的受限准入预测一致。

Abstract: Intent-based decentralized exchanges delegate execution to a competitive class of agents -- solvers -- whose behavior is shaped by protocol-designed reward rules. We measure how a change to those rules reshapes who captures value, using a governance-dated natural experiment: CoW Protocol CIP-74 (effective 8 December 2025), which replaced a fixed solver-reward cap with one tied to protocol revenue and introduced an ad-valorem volume fee. Using daily solver shares over 395 days, we find the reform reallocated trading value by order size. The robust signature is a monotone size gradient: concentration fell in small orders and rose in large ones across four order-value buckets (Spearman rho=1.00, exact permutation p=0.042) -- a pattern that survives dropping the single largest solver. Aggregate concentration also rose (volume-weighted HHI 0.176->0.241), substantially carried by the incumbent top solver. By trade count the market de-concentrated (count-HHI -0.060). A simple solver-economics model rationalizes the pattern: an ad-valorem fee is competitively neutral, while a revenue-linked reward cap raises the marginal payoff to inventory-rich solvers on large orders -- consistent with restricted-entry predictions (Chitra et al. 2024). A control venue (UniswapX) shows no matching break. We detect no change in average execution quality (~7 bps bound). A triple-difference exploiting a February 2026 fee cut is directionally consistent but underpowered. Reward design measurably reallocates who captures value in intent markets, without moving the average price users receive.

</details>


### [95] [Ethereum NFT Smart Contracts: Knowledge-Guided Vulnerability Detection with LLM and Code Slicing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21983)
*Deyu Yang,Rundong Wei,Xiaoqi Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种结合漏洞聚焦代码切片、ERC-721知识库和约束DeepSeek分析的NFT智能合约漏洞检测方法，在450个合约样本上达到97.1%的正标签率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态分析器对应用特定逻辑检测效果有限，而大语言模型分析易被无关代码干扰且输出不一致，需要一种高效、可靠的NFT合约漏洞检测方法。

Method: 使用正则表达式定位重入、整数溢出、时间戳依赖的候选语句；通过结构感知的上下文窗口算法提取带行号的代码切片；结合ERC-721知识库和显式决策规则，由DeepSeek按固定输出模式分析。

Result: 完整配置下正标签率97.1%；去除外部知识库降至87.11%；去除知识库并分析完整合约降至73.78%。

Conclusion: 聚焦的代码上下文和领域约束对检测器的输出有实质性影响，该方法能有效提升NFT合约漏洞检测的准确性和可靠性。

Abstract: Ethereum non-fungible tokens (NFTs) implement ownership, transfer, authorization, and metadata operations through smart contracts, making contract vulnerabilities a direct risk to digital assets. Existing static analyzers provide efficient rule-based screening but can struggle with application-specific logic, whereas unconstrained large language model analysis may be distracted by irrelevant code or produce inconsistent outputs. We present a vulnerability-detection method that combines vulnerability-focused code slicing, an ERC-721-oriented knowledge base, and constrained DeepSeek analysis. Regular-expression patterns locate candidate statements for reentrancy, integer overflow or underflow, and timestamp dependence. A structure-aware context-window algorithm then extracts line-numbered code slices. DeepSeek analyzes each slice using explicit decision rules and a fixed output schema, and the resulting records support automated batch processing. On 450 NFT contract samples, the full configuration produced 437 positive labels, corresponding to a reported positive-label rate of 97.1%. Removing the external knowledge base reduced this rate to 87.11%, while analyzing complete contracts without the knowledge base reduced it to 73.78%. These results indicate that focused code context and domain constraints materially affect the detector's reported output.

</details>


### [96] [Practical Graph Optimisation and AI-Driven Models for Active Directory Security Hardening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22009)
*Huy Q. Ngo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对微软Active Directory攻击图的安全防御问题，提出了四个基于博弈论和优化的决策模型，包括蜜罐/诱饵部署、动态防御、自适应优先级排序和端到端自适应策略，所有模型的计算复杂度均为NP难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AD攻击图防御方案存在三个不足：1）假设图是静态的，而真实AD环境高度动态；2）仅限于撤销漏洞（边移除）的被动防御，主动防御机制研究不足；3）缺乏系统管理员反馈纳入优先级排序的端到端模型。

Method: 1）基于最小化最短路径数量和域管理员可达节点数量的蜜罐/诱饵部署模型；2）考虑AD图动态/时间特性的防御策略，以最大化最坏情况下的响应时间；3）向IT管理员查询高风险攻击路径的自适应优先级模型；4）通过寻找泛化系统管理员决策的自适应边移除策略，最小化管理审批工作量的端到端模型。

Result: 所提出的四个模型对应的底层问题均为计算上不可处理的（NP难）。

Conclusion: 论文通过博弈论和优化方法，提出了面向动态AD攻击图的主动防御与自适应优先级决策框架，为实际AD安全防护提供了理论支撑，但所有模型面临计算复杂度的挑战。

Abstract: Microsoft's Active Directory (AD) is a directory service that enables the IT admin to manage security permissions and control access within a Windows domain network. As a core management system in many of organisation, AD has become a primary target for adversaries. While many solutions for hardening attack graphs exist, these efforts fall short in addressing several key practical challenges specific to the AD attack graph. First, existing models often assume the graph is static, whereas a real-world AD environment is highly dynamic. Second, most proposed solutions are limited to the defensive measure of revoking vulnerabilities (edge removal), while more active defence mechanisms are largely unstudied. Third, because not all remediations are implementable, a practical end-to-end model must incorporate system admin feedback into the prioritisation process. This thesis aims to address these limitations by studying and proposing a number of game-theoretic and optimisation-based decision-making models. First, we propose a honeypot/decoy placement model based on the principle of minimising the number of shortest paths and the number of Domain Admin-reachable nodes. Second, building on this model, we introduce a defence strategy that considers the dynamic/temporal nature of the AD graph, where the objective is to find the location to deploy decoys that maximises the worst-case incident response time. Third, we introduce an adaptive prioritisation model that queries each high-risk attack path to the IT administrator for mediation. Finally, we introduce an end-to-end adaptive prioritisation model that minimises the approval effort of the system admin by finding a general adaptive edge-removal policy that generalises the system admin's decisions to edges with similar risk features. We show that the problems underlying all of the contributed models are computationally intractable.

</details>


### [97] [Agent Security Needs Redefinition through a Holistic Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22024)
*Vincent Siu,Jingxuan He,Kyle Montgomery,Zhun Wang,Chenguang Wang,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文论证智能体安全本质上是上下文问题，而非当前主流的内容安全问题。通过源授权、任务对齐、行动对齐和数据隔离四个维度重新定义安全评估框架，指出基于内容的评估存在结构性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体安全研究将安全视为动作内容问题（判断指令是否恶意），但同样的动作在上下文中可能是合法管理请求或恶意注入攻击，内容本身无法区分。作者旨在纠正这一系统性误定义。

Method: 提出上下文安全四属性框架：源授权（谁发出指令）、任务对齐（授权目标）、行动对齐（动作是否服务于目标）、数据隔离（跨权限边界的信息流），强调四个属性需联合评估且持续检测。

Result: 间接提示注入被重新定义为源授权违反；快照基准测试无法评估数据隔离；现有防御可归入四个属性的近似实现。上下文框架改变了哪些防御是合理的、哪些评估有意义、哪些攻击模式能被检测到。

Conclusion: 智能体安全应从基于内容的评估转向持续上下文评估，四属性框架为安全设计、评估和攻击检测提供了更准确的理论基础。

Abstract: Agent security is widely treated as a question about action content. Defenses ask whether an instruction looks malicious. Benchmarks ask whether an agent performs a harmful sounding action. \textbf{We argue that agent security is fundamentally a contextual problem, and that the current content based framing systematically misdefines it.} A command to ``delete user data'' might be a routine administrative request or a prompt injection attacking production systems, and the content alone cannot distinguish the two. Authorization context can. Across every injection task in AgentDojo and WASP, the same action is one an authenticated user would plausibly request in a routine workflow, which makes the conflation a structural property of evaluating security through content.
  We operationalize contextual security through four properties that must hold jointly and be evaluated continuously across the agent's trajectory. Source Authorization asks who issued the command. Task Alignment specifies the agent's authorized objective. Action Alignment evaluates whether each action serves that objective. Data Isolation governs information flows across privilege boundaries. Under this reframing, indirect prompt injection becomes a Source Authorization violation. Snapshot benchmarks are structurally incapable of evaluating Data Isolation. Existing defenses are reorganized around the property they actually approximate. The contextual reframing changes which defenses are coherent, which evaluations measure something useful, and which attack patterns evaluation can see at all.

</details>


### [98] [BioZKFHE: Scalable Encrypted Biometric Identification via Verifiable Homomorphic Similarity Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22065)
*Rundong Xin,Taotao Wang,Xiaoxiao Wu,Weizhi Meng,Shengli Zhang,Shui Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BioZKFHE是一个结合BGV同态加密与委员会验证的框架，通过SCMV打包减少存储开销、PVSC并行证明加速验证，实现了大规模加密生物识别的可验证性，在FaceNet/MobileFaceNet上达到近无损效用、67%存储压缩和22-44秒端到端运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于FHE的生物识别系统主要关注机密性，但缺乏对加密相似度输出正确性的可验证性，而可验证性在旋转操作、带宽开销和重复同态轨迹证明方面存在严重瓶颈。

Method: 提出两个核心方法：(1) SCMV打包，通过基T展开将多个量化嵌入值绑定到每个明文条目，减少加密存储并避免旋转操作；(2) PVSC，利用BGV的Double-CRT结构将块状相似度轨迹分解为并行证明实例，结合委员会中间证明打开/解密和智能合约验证。

Result: 在FaceNet和MobileFaceNet上实现近无损生物识别效用，加密存储减少67%，10k到40k模板的端到端证明验证运行时间约22-44秒。

Conclusion: BioZKFHE在标准格假设和委员会/验证者假设下，同时保障了机密性、加密输出完整性和最终结果完整性，为大规模外包加密生物识别提供了实用的可验证同态相似度计算方案。

Abstract: Large-scale biometric identification in outsourced settings requires two properties simultaneously: biometric templates and queries must remain protected during computation, and the encrypted similarity outputs produced by an untrusted compute node must be verifiably correct before any application result is released. Existing FHE-based biometric systems primarily address confidentiality, while practical verifiability introduces two bottlenecks in the underlying encrypted 1:N matching layer: rotation- and bandwidth-heavy similarity evaluation and the high cost of proving repeated homomorphic similarity traces. We present BioZKFHE, a framework for scalable encrypted biometric identification via verifiable homomorphic similarity evaluation that combines BGV homomorphic computation with committee-mediated proof opening/decryption and smart-contract verification of opened proof batches. To reduce encrypted storage and avoid rotation-heavy encrypted 1:N matching, we propose Single-Coefficient Multi-Value (SCMV) packing, which binds multiple quantized embedding values into each plaintext entry through base-T expansion. To make proof generation practical, we propose Parallelizable and Verifiable Similarity Computation (PVSC), which exploits the Double-CRT execution structure of BGV to decompose each blockwise similarity trace into parallel proof instances that are opened and checked before result release. Under standard lattice assumptions and explicit committee/verifier assumptions, we analyze recoverability, noise growth, confidentiality, encrypted-output integrity, and finalized-result integrity. Experiments on FaceNet and MobileFaceNet show near-lossless biometric utility, up to 67 percent encrypted-storage reduction, and about 22 to 44 seconds end-to-end proof-verified runtime for 10k to 40k templates.

</details>


### [99] [PoCEvolve: Generating Proof-of-Concept Exploits from Security Patches with Vulnerability-Aware Prompt Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22076)
*Duc Manh Tran,Ratnadira Widyasari,Ivana Clairine Irsan,Huihui Huang,Ting Zhang,Shar Lwin Khin,Ouh Eng Lieh,Hong Jin Kang,David Lo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PoCEvolve框架，能从漏洞修复提交直接生成PoC漏洞利用代码，在无详细漏洞报告的情况下达到58.4%的成功率，相比现有方法提升20.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漏洞修复提交与详细漏洞报告之间存在长时间延迟，导致防御方在补丁公开后缺乏可执行证据（如PoC）来评估风险、确定优先级和验证修复效果。

Method: 提出PoCEvolve，一种漏洞感知的提示进化框架。它从漏洞修复提交出发，推断易受攻击的API和代码覆盖率信息，通过评估不同维度上下文的有用性来引导提示进化，从而生成更有效的漏洞利用提示。

Result: PoCEvolve在SecBench.VFC.js数据集上达到58.4%的PoC生成成功率，相对PoCGen提升20.7%，相对GPT-4o-mini基线提升200.0%；使用Qwen3.7-Plus模型可达85.3%。

Conclusion: PoCEvolve能够有效填补漏洞修复与详细报告之间的信息鸿沟，无需预先存在的详细漏洞报告即可生成可执行的PoC，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Ideally, the detailed information about a vulnerability should be made available together with the fixing commit. In practice, however, such details often become available only long after the commit, even when a CVE has already been published. During this window, the patch is already public, so attackers can reverse-engineer it, yet defenders lack the details needed to assess exposure, prioritize, and validate the fix. Executable evidence, such as a proof-of-concept (PoC) exploit, could fill this gap. Prior work has automated PoC generation, but the state-of-the-art approach, PoCGen, assumes that a detailed vulnerability report is already available, which is precisely what is missing during this window. In this paper, we first present an empirical study quantifying the long delay between the fixing commit and the availability of a detailed vulnerability report. We then introduce PoCEvolve, a vulnerability-aware prompt-evolution framework that generates PoCs directly from vulnerability-fixing commits. Given a vulnerability-fixing commit, PoCEvolve synthesizes a corresponding PoC exploit. To learn from unsuccessful generation attempts, PoCEvolve assesses the usefulness of different dimensions of vulnerability-related context, including the inferred vulnerable API and code-coverage information. These assessments guide prompt evolution towards more effective exploit-generation prompts. We evaluate PoCEvolve on SecBench.VFC.js, where PoCEvolve achieves a PoC generation success rate of 58.4%, corresponding to relative improvements of 20.7% over PoCGen and 200.0% over the LLM baseline with GPT-4o-mini. With a recent model, Qwen3.7-Plus, PoCEvolve achieves a higher success rate of 85.3%. When detailed vulnerability reports are available, PoCEvolve achieves a success rate of 71.7%, improving over PoCGen by 11.1%.

</details>


### [100] [Transforming Keystroke Noise to Text: Self-Supervised Acoustic Eavesdropping Attacks on Keyboards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22094)
*Atsunori Okada,Akira Ito,Rei Ueno,Yuichi Hayashi,Naofumi Homma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自监督声学窃听攻击方法，仅通过按键声音就能重构输入文本，无需目标设备的标注数据，在近距离、远距离、隔墙和在线会议等场景下均达到90%以上的重构准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有声学侧信道攻击依赖目标设备的标注数据，难以在无监督场景下实现高精度文本重构，而实际中窃听者往往无法获取此类标注数据，因此需要一种无需标注、可跨设备泛化的自监督方法。

Method: 结合无监督声学聚类、基于Transformer的语言模型推理和迭代自训练，在声学到字符映射高度不确定的情况下实现稳定的字符推断。

Result: 近距离（手机靠近目标设备）100-150次按键即可达到99%以上重构准确率；远距离（约3米）、隔墙、在线会议场景下150-250次按键即可达到90%以上准确率。

Conclusion: 基于音频的按键文本重构在无标注数据、有限观测样本的条件下是实际可行的，揭示了此前被低估的隐私风险。

Abstract: We present a self-supervised acoustic eavesdropping attack that reconstructs typed text solely from keystroke sounds, without requiring labeled data for the target device. The proposed attack enables stealthy eavesdropping in two real-world scenarios-physical spaces (public and semi-public) and online meetings. Our method combines unsupervised acoustic clustering with Transformer-based language model inference and iterative self-training, enabling stable character inference under highly uncertain acoustic-to-character mappings. We demonstrate that the proposed method achieves over 99% reconstruction accuracy with only 100-150 observed keystrokes under a close-proximity recording setup using a smartphone placed near the target device, significantly outperforming prior unsupervised baselines in low-data regimes. We further evaluate robustness across multiple laptop platforms and in realistic acquisition channels, including distance recording from approximately 3 meters away on the same desk, through-the-wall eavesdropping with a contact microphone, and background keyboard noise in online conferencing systems. Across these scenarios, the proposed method achieves high reconstruction accuracy (often exceeding 90%) with approximately 150-250 observed keystrokes. These results indicate that accurate text reconstruction from keystroke sounds is feasible in practice under an audio-only setting, even with limited observed keystrokes and without requiring device-specific labeled data, highlighting a realistic and previously underestimated privacy risk.

</details>


### [101] [DeFiScreener: Efficient DeFi Attack Pre-screening in Smart Contracts via Historical Case Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22184)
*Rui Cao,Shaojing Fan,Zhimei Sui,Liming Fang,Ziqi Yang,Yingying Jiao,Zhenguang Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DeFiScreener，首个利用历史攻击案例对DeFi攻击进行自动预筛查的框架，通过函数调用树、LLM语义嵌入和双层筛选（函数级+序列级），在207个真实DeFi攻击数据集上达到98.55%召回率和84.30%精确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DeFi检测工具通常只覆盖特定攻击类型，检测覆盖率严重不足。本文认为有效解决这一差距的方法是从大量智能合约函数和调用序列中预筛查易受攻击的实例，这源于作者发现的"危险时间不对称"现象。

Method: 1) 构建函数调用树(FCT)；2) 使用大语言模型(LLM)生成每个函数的语义嵌入；3) 函数级：将函数嵌入与历史攻击模式库匹配；4) 序列级：提出攻击模式导向的蒙特卡洛树搜索(APO-MCTS)高效探索FCT；5) 候选结果交由LLM进行解释和安全分析。

Result: 在包含207个真实DeFi攻击事件的数据集上，DeFiScreener实现了98.55%的召回率和84.30%的精确率。

Conclusion: DeFiScreener作为一种自动化预筛查框架，能够有效识别易受攻击的函数和调用序列，显著提升DeFi安全检测的覆盖范围。

Abstract: Blockchain and its killer applications, particularly decentralized finance (DeFi), are gaining widespread adoption, with over 5,200 DeFi projects deployed on mainstream blockchains as of January 2026. At the same time, security risks in DeFi are becoming increasingly serious. However, existing DeFi detection tools usually cover only specific attack types, exhibiting severely limited detection coverage.
  In this paper, we argue that an effective way to address this gap is to pre-screen vulnerable instances from large volumes of smart contract functions and call sequences. This is motivated by a key phenomenon we term "perilous temporal asymmetry". Inspired by this, we propose DeFiScreener, the first automated pre-screening framework for DeFi attacks that uses historical exploit cases to identify potentially vulnerable functions and call sequences. Given the full source code of a target project, DeFiScreener builds Function Call Trees (FCTs) and generates semantic embeddings for each function using a large language model (LLM), allowing both program structure and function intent to be analyzed together. It then applies a dual-level screening process. At the function level, function embeddings are matched against an Attack Pattern Library of historically exploited functions. At the sequence level, the proposed Attack Pattern Oriented Monte Carlo Tree Search (APO-MCTS) efficiently explores the FCTs and screens vulnerable call sequences. The identified candidates are ultimately passed to an LLM for further interpretive and security analysis.
  We empirically evaluate the DeFiScreener over datasets comprising 207 real-world DeFi attack incidents. Experimental results demonstrate that DeFiScreener achieves a remarkable 98.55% recall and 84.30% precision in attack pre-screening.

</details>


### [102] [trasgoDP: An Open Source Framework for Releasing Noised Tabular Microdata under Local Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22230)
*Judith Sáinz-Pardo Díaz,Álvaro López García*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: trasgoDP是一个模块化、开源、易用的Python框架，用于在本地差分隐私和地理不可区分性保证下发布表格微观数据和位置数据，支持隐私-效用权衡的系统性探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私在聚合数据上研究广泛，但在行级微观数据发布方面缺乏可复用的软件工具，位置数据的度量隐私工具更是匮乏。

Method: 实现了数值/分类属性的本地DP机制（拉普拉斯、高斯、指数、随机响应）、位置数据的地理不可区分性机制，以及一套效用度量指标（含新颖的相关性损失度量）。

Result: 提供了一个可复现、开源基线，用于在正式本地差分隐私保证下评估表格和位置数据发布方法。

Conclusion: trasgoDP填补了微观数据本地差分隐私可复用软件工具的空白，为研究社区提供了标准化评估平台。

Abstract: trasgoDP is a modular, open-source, and easy-to-use Python framework for releasing tabular microdata under ε-local differential privacy guarantees, as well as location data under geo-indistinguishability assumptions, designed to be installed and integrated within standard data science workflows. The software enables systematic exploration of privacy-utility trade-offs across multiple mechanisms, data types, and ε values. While differential privacy has been extensively studied for aggregate data, its application to row-wise microdata release remains underexploited in terms of reusable software tools, a gap that is even more pronounced in the case of metric privacy and location-based data. trasgoDP implements local-DP mechanisms for numerical and categorical attributes (Laplace, Gaussian, Exponential, and Randomized Response), a geo-indistinguishability mechanism for location data, and a set of utility metrics, including a novel correlation-loss measure, to quantify information loss as a function of the allocated privacy budget. The objective of this work is to provide the research community with a reproducible, open-source baseline for evaluating tabular and location-based data publication methodologies under formal local differential privacy guarantees.

</details>


### [103] [Kalyna Block Cipher: From Design Space Exploration to ASIC Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22269)
*Carlos Gewehr,Levent Aksoy,Mariia Rodinko,Roman Oliynykov,Samuel Pagliarini*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出乌克兰加密标准Kalyna的硬件加速器实现，在65nm ASIC上探索了面积-延迟权衡，并集成了旁路分析和故障注入攻击的防护措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kalyna作为乌克兰国家加密标准，此前仅有软件实现而缺乏硬件加速器，为了填补这一空白并满足不同应用场景对面积、延迟和能耗的差异化需求。

Method: 探索多种设计架构实现加密/解密/统一加解密功能，采用硬件缩减技术、隐藏/掩码/冗余等侧信道与故障注入攻击防护措施，并在65nm ASIC芯片上验证。

Result: 成功在65nm ASIC上实现多种Kalyna硬件设计，验证了不同设计空间搜索的必要性；随机化执行可隐藏功耗侧信道泄漏，时间冗余可缓解故障注入攻击。

Conclusion: 提出的Kalyna硬件架构能够有效权衡面积与延迟，同时集成安全防护机制，为乌克兰加密标准的实际部署提供了可行的硬件解决方案。

Abstract: The Kalyna block cipher is a Ukrainian cryptography standard, selected through a national competition held between 2007 and 2010 and approved in 2015. Although its software implementations have been introduced, hardware-efficient implementations of the algorithm, i.e., accelerators, do not exist. In this paper, we explore various design architectures to implement its encryption, decryption, and unified encryption/decryption functions, considering the trade-off between area and latency. We present hardware reduction techniques and introduce alternative designs with low area, latency, and energy consumption, targeting an application-specific integrated circuit (ASIC). We present hardware-efficient designs that include countermeasures against side-channel analysis (SCA) and fault injection (FI) attacks, such as hiding, masking, and duplication techniques. We validate these implementations in a 65\;nm ASIC chip. Experimental results confirm the need for alternative designs that explore the design search space for different requirements. The proposed architectures enable hiding the power SCA leakage by randomizing the execution of operations, and the temporal duplication in designs with countermeasures against the SCA attacks can mitigate the FI attacks. The functionality of the ASIC test chip, including various Kalyna designs, is validated through measurements.

</details>


### [104] [A Maximum Entropy Implementation of Differential Privacy Under Linear Invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22450)
*Ryan Lafferty,Anindya Roy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在聚合约束下实现高熵差分隐私的方法，确保约束以概率1或极接近1成立，并推导了相应的隐私保证，部分解决了相关矩阵零空间的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私实现（如高斯或拉普拉斯噪声）通常假设噪声独立，但在实际应用中存在必须遵守的聚合约束（如美国十年人口普查中的州总计数），这些约束导致噪声不再独立，传统的差分隐私保证需要重新评估。

Method: 提出一种高熵差分隐私实现方法，以概率1或指数级接近1的概率维持聚合不变量，并从理论上推导在该约束下的隐私保证，部分解决了关于相关矩阵零空间的开放问题。该方法还可推广到线性等式约束下的正态混合模型采样场景。

Result: 所提方法能够在存在聚合约束的条件下维持不变量，并提供了相应的差分隐私理论保证；同时部分解决了相关矩阵零空间的开放理论问题。

Conclusion: 该工作为在必须遵守法定聚合约束的场景下实施差分隐私提供了理论支撑，且方法具有更广泛的统计应用价值。

Abstract: Differential privacy is the standard for ensuring data privacy and is widely used in major data publications, including reporting results from the U.S. decennial census. Common implementation of differential privacy uses independent Gaussian or Laplace noise addition to the database. However, there could be aggregate (linear) queries to the database that are excluded from the privacy budget, for example, state totals that can not be perturbed due to constitutional mandates. Any implementation of a differential privacy is required to honor these constraints, also referred to as invariants. Under aggregation constraints, the noise vector is no longer independent and the traditional differential privacy guarantees have to be re-evaluated. We propose a high entropy differential privacy implementation that maintains the aggregation invariants with probability one or exponentially close to one and derive the privacy guarantees for the implementation under the invariants. The theoretical proof covers a partial solution to an open question about the null space of correlation matrices. Moreover, the methodology has general use in the context of sampling from normal mixture models under linear equality constraints.

</details>


### [105] [Broken Object Level Authorization in the Wild: An Empirical Taxonomy from 100+ Bug Bounty Disclosures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25865)
*Bandana Kaur*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对HackerOne上200份IDOR/访问控制漏洞报告进行系统分析，发现78.5%确认为BOLA漏洞，其中Action-Level Object BOLA占41.7%，并指出标签计数会高估BOLA信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BOLA虽被列为最关键的API安全漏洞，但现有研究几乎完全是概念性的，缺乏大规模实证分析。

Method: 构建200份HackerOne漏洞报告的抽样框架，应用三重筛选标准得到107份分类报告，采用LLM辅助的模式补全程序进行六类BOLA分类。

Result: 107份报告中84份(78.5%)确认为BOLA；Action-Level Object BOLA占41.7%，与Direct Object Reference BOLA并列两大主要类型；21.5%报告超出严格范围；发现11.9%的垂直权限提升漏洞和GraphQL Global ID的系统性利用。

Conclusion: 研究结果对API安全测试协议、开发者教育和OWASP指南具有直接指导意义。

Abstract: Broken Object Level Authorization (BOLA) is consistently ranked the most critical API security vulnerability, yet the existing literature remains almost entirely conceptual. This paper presents one of the first large-scale empirical analyses of BOLA in publicly disclosed bug bounty reports. We constructed a reproducible sampling frame of 200 HackerOne disclosures tagged IDOR or Improper Access Control (2021-2026) and applied a three-criterion inclusion filter, yielding 107 fully classified reports. Classification used an LLM-assisted schema-completion procedure under constrained, human-adjudicated criteria against a six-family BOLA taxonomy. Of 107 classified reports, 84 (78.5%) were confirmed in-scope BOLA. Action-Level Object BOLA, defined by unauthorized state-changing actions on another user's objects, accounts for 41.7% of confirmed cases and emerges alongside Direct Object Reference BOLA as one of the two dominant families observed in the dataset. This shows a pattern historically underrepresented in practitioner guidance. Approximately 21.5% of classified reports are out-of-scope under strict criteria, indicating that tag-counting on platforms like HackerOne significantly overstates the BOLA-specific signal. We report distributions across family, action type, authorization direction, industry sector, identifier format, and exploit mechanism. Key secondary findings include an 11.9% rate of vertical (user-to-admin) privilege failures and systematic exploitation of GraphQL Global IDs across major platforms. Findings have direct implications for API security testing protocols, developer education, and OWASP guidance.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [106] [From Obligation to Specification: A Survey on Validating EU AI Act Requirements in RE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21608)
*T. Y. Emmy Lai,Sven Giesselbach,Matthias Koch,Héctor Allende-Cid*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 针对欧盟AI法案下的需求工程，研究发现组织普遍缺乏系统化流程，LLM智能体验证工具被视为有前景的辅助手段，但需建立安全防护机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟AI法案生效后，组织需将监管义务转化为可测试、可审计的需求，但目前缺乏系统化流程，亟需探索有效的工具和方法来弥合这一差距。

Method: 采用混合方法探索性研究，包括专家访谈(N=10)和在线问卷(N=15)，参与者涵盖需求工程、数据科学、开发和合规等角色。

Result: 尽管欧盟AI法案被认为高度相关，但结构化机制普遍缺失。LLM工具被认为有助于映射义务、评估覆盖率和组织证据，但参与者对完全自动化表示担忧，强调需要安全防护。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，提出了面向欧盟AI法案合规的闭环方法的最小需求。

Abstract: With the EU AI Act entering into force, organizations developing or operating AI systems face new obligations on transparency, risk management, and traceability. For Requirements Engineering (RE), these obligations must be translated into testable, auditable requirements and verifiable evidence. However, many organizations currently lack systematic processes to achieve this. We hypothesize that LLM-based agentic validation tools can support this translation, thereby helping to close this gap. We present a mixed-method exploratory study with expert interviews (N=10) and an online survey (N=15) to assess organizational preparedness for EU AI Act-oriented RE and perceptions of LLM-based, agentic closed-loop validation tools, with participants spanning RE, data science, development, and compliance roles. Our results show that, although the EU AI Act is viewed as highly relevant, structured mechanisms to capture regulatory obligations, propagate updates into projects, and maintain lifecycle-wide traceability and evidence are often missing. Participants see LLM-based tools as promising for mapping obligations to requirements, assessing coverage, and organizing evidence, but express strong concerns about full automation and stress the need for safeguards. Based on these findings, we outline minimum requirements for an EU AI Act-ready closed-loop approach.

</details>


### [107] [Tool-Guided Retrieval-Augmented Repair for Securing LLM-Generated C Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21641)
*Vidyut Sriram,Saatvik Pradhan,Suman Saha*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结合编译诊断、CodeQL静态分析和KLEE符号执行的“分析-修复”工作流，通过迭代反馈和检索修复模式，显著降低了LLM生成C代码的编译失败率和安全缺陷率。在5000个嵌入式编程任务上，CodeLlama 7B的安全缺陷率从49%降至19%，DeepSeek Coder 1.3B的编译失败率从42%降至22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的C代码常存在安全漏洞和编译错误，对嵌入式及资源受限系统构成风险，亟需提升代码的可靠性和安全性。

Method: 提出一种分析-修复工作流，集成编译诊断、CodeQL静态分析和KLEE符号执行，并结合检索历史修复模式进行迭代优化。

Result: 在5000个嵌入式C编程任务上，CodeLlama 7B安全缺陷率从49%降至19%，CodeQL错误减少83.7%；DeepSeek Coder 1.3B编译失败率从42%降至22%，安全缺陷率从35%降至15%。

Conclusion: 集成轻量级分析工具可以有效提升LLM生成代码在嵌入式开发中的安全性，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language models can generate C code from natural-language descriptions, but resulting programs often contain security vulnerabilities and compilation errors, posing risks for embedded and resource-constrained systems. This work investigates how feedback and retrieval improve reliability of LLM-generated C code. We present an analysis-and-repair workflow that combines compilation diagnostics, CodeQL static analysis, and KLEE symbolic execution with retrieval of prior repair patterns for iterative refinement.
  Evaluated on 5,000 C programming tasks exercising embedded relevant vulnerabilities, baseline models show substantial reliability gaps, with compilation failure rates up to 46% and security defect rates up to 49%. Our approach improves both metrics. For CodeLlama 7B, security defect rates decrease from 49% to 19% and total CodeQL errors drop from 15,088 to 2,463 (83.7%). For DeepSeek Coder 1.3B, compilation failures are reduced from 42% to 22% and security defects from 35% to 15%. These results show that integrating lightweight analysis tools can improve the safety of LLM-generated code for embedded development.

</details>


### [108] [Vibe Coding in Software Development: A Multivocal Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21652)
*Shahbaz Siddeeq,Muhammad Waseem,Kai-Kristian Kemell,Mika Saari,Jussi Rasku,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对"Vibe Coding"（自然语言编程）进行了多声道文献综述，整合了47篇同行评审和灰色文献，发现Vibe Coding并非一次性提示，而是迭代的生成-评估-修订循环，开发者角色从编码转向规范、监督和验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于Vibe Coding的定义、实践和治理知识分散在学术和实践来源中，现有综述尚未整合这两种证据流。

Method: 采用多声道文献综述方法，按照既定指南对同行评审和灰色文献进行检索（2022年至2025年10月），经过筛选、可信度评估和滚雪球法后保留47篇文献（28篇同行评审，19篇灰色文献），通过描述性映射和主题综合进行跨八个研究问题的分析。

Result: 21/47篇文献（45%）报告了短期生产力和原型开发时间方面的提升；可维护性、长期质量和防护措施有效性的证据有限；原型设计和UI工作的证据最强，生产环境、数据密集型和安全关键场景的证据最弱；工具可见性不等于有效性。

Conclusion: 这是首批在单一文档协议下整合Vibe Coding同行评审和灰色文献的综述之一。未来工作应评估防护措施有效性、研究会话级动态和长期可维护性，并在生产、数据密集型和安全关键场景中测试Vibe Coding。

Abstract: Vibe coding is a software development practice in which developers state intent in natural language and large language models generate code. It is often framed as one-shot prompting, but the evidence describes an intent-driven, iterative workflow whose outcomes depend on how generated code is evaluated and governed. Knowledge of how vibe coding is defined, practiced, and governed is scattered across academic and practitioner sources, and, to our knowledge, existing reviews have not yet integrated both evidence streams. We conducted a multivocal literature review of peer-reviewed and grey literature following established guidelines. Searches spanned 2022 to October 2025. After screening, credibility assessment, and snowballing, 47 sources were retained (28 peer-reviewed and 19 grey) and analyzed through descriptive mapping and thematic synthesis across eight research questions. Vibe coding is consistently described as an iterative generation-evaluation-revision loop rather than a one-shot activity, and developer work shifts from writing code towards specification, supervision, and validation. Short-term productivity and time-to-prototype gains are reported in 21 of 47 sources (45%), while evidence on maintainability, long-term quality, and safeguard effectiveness remains limited. Evidence is strongest for prototyping and user-interface work and weakest for production, data-intensive, and safety-critical use, and tool visibility does not imply effectiveness. This is one of the first reviews to integrate peer-reviewed and grey literature on vibe coding under a single documented protocol. Future work should evaluate safeguard effectiveness, study session-level dynamics and long-term maintainability, and test vibe coding in production, data-intensive, and safety-critical settings.

</details>


### [109] [Cross-Model LLM Code Review: Should you use Claude to review Codex or vice versa?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21656)
*Zuodong Xiang,Yike Zhang,YueMing Zhang,Hailu Xu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 实验表明，用Claude审查Codex生成的代码可将通过率从71.6%提升至89.7%，而反向（Codex审查Claude）反而降低性能，最佳配对是"Claude审查Codex"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者常使用双代理模式（一个写草稿、一个审查），但该配对策略是否值得成本与时间，以及配对顺序是否重要尚不明确。

Method: 在116个LCB任务上，使用Claude和Codex在六种条件下进行受控实验：双基线、跨模型配对、同模型配对。审查者看到问题和草稿但无法执行测试，模拟代码审查流程。

Result: Claude审查Codex：通过率从71.6%提升至89.7%（p=0.001）；Codex自我审查：提升至84.5%（p=0.022）；Codex审查Claude：从91.4%降至82.8%（p=0.046）；Claude自我审查：无变化。

Conclusion: 有用的配对是非对称的：应用Claude审查Codex，而非相反方向。

Abstract: Developers increasingly use two coding agents together: one writes a draft, and the other reviews it. However, it is not clear whether the pairing is worth its cost and time, or whether the order of the pairing matters. We run a controlled experiment on 116 recent hard and medium lcb tasks with Claude and Codex across six conditions to approximate a software practitioner's workflow: both solo baselines, both cross-model orderings, and both same-model orderings. The reviewer sees the problem and the writer's draft but cannot execute tests, which approximates a code review step. Claude review raises Codex drafts from 71.6% to 89.7% ($p_{BH}=.001$); Codex self review raises them to 84.5% ($p_{BH}=.022$). The reverse direction does not pay off: Codex reviewing Claude drafts drops the pass rate from 91.4% to 82.8% ($p_{BH}=.046$), and Claude self review leaves the 91.4% baseline unchanged. Our evaluation indicates that the useful pairing is asymmetric: use Claude to review Codex, not the other way around.

</details>


### [110] [Pixels for Programs? A Cross-Provider Case Study of Input-Token Accounting for Source Code as Text and Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21672)
*Ronak Bhalgami*

Main category: cs.SE
